# Requirement Traceability Matrix (rebuilt)

Generated by `prompts/10_TEST_CATALOG_AUDIT_AND_REBUILD.md`, NO-ASSUMPTION MODE. Supersedes the old
workbook's own `RTM` sheet (23 rows, organized by the old blueprint's `M01`–`M2x` modules) for
traceability purposes — that sheet is retained as historical input, not reused as current truth,
since its module boundaries and requirement IDs predate this project's confirmed scope changes.

Full chain per `CLAUDE.md`: **Evidence → Decision → BRD → PRD Requirement → Feature ID → Acceptance
Criteria → UX/Screen → Architecture/DB/API/RBAC/Integration → Test → Code → Release Evidence.**
This matrix covers the **Feature ID → AC → contract → test** segment; the Evidence→Decision→BRD→PRD
segment is already covered by `docs/features/FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md`.

**Addendum, 2026-09-14** — a new "School" section added after this document's 2026-09-01 approval,
per `DEC-SCOPE-011`/`012` and `DEC-ROLE-006`. Original sections/content elsewhere are unchanged.

## How to read this matrix

Rather than one row per individual `-AC0#` (68 features × ~3 ACs each ≈ 200+ rows — too large to be
useful as a static document, and it would duplicate `FEATURE_ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA.md`), this matrix is
one row per **Feature ID**, listing: contract documents it depends on, its old-catalogue test
coverage status, and its new/rewritten test coverage. Full test case detail is in `test_audit.json`
(old cases) and `TEST_REWRITE_PLAN.md` (new/rewritten cases).

Added `sync_enquiry_to_crm_task` (Celery, exponential backoff, max 5 retries) and switched `create_enquiry` to dispatch it, matching `INTEGRATION_CONTRACTS.md` §1's outbox pattern as originally specified. Backend evidence retained: 7 pytest cases (historic 45/45 suite). Current focused E2E: 6/6 — search/empty recovery, category filtering + URL state, full detail rendering, per-page SEO title, graceful 404, 375px responsiveness/no horizontal overflow. Current production web build and focused ESLint/typecheck pass. |
| `PUB-004` | `API_CONTRACT.md` §2, `DATA_MODEL.md` §2.4 (corrected) | `KEEP` (capacity rule open, per feature's own AC02) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-02, Wave 2): reused the existing `Event` model for listing rather than the `Webinar` table `DATA_MODEL.md` §2.4 originally assumed carried over (same correction pattern as `PUB-001`'s `CareerPath`/`RealProject`); added the genuinely-missing piece, net-new `WebinarRegistration` (alembic `0009`) — `Event.registration_url` was only ever an external link, no in-app registration capture existed anywhere before this. Past-webinar registration rejected server-side (409), not just hidden in the UI. Backend: 7 new pytest cases (143/143 suite). E2E: 5 new Playwright cases (72/72 suite) — list+detail rendering, 404 handling, no-auth registration, nav discoverability, 375px responsive |
| `PUB-005` | `API_CONTRACT.md` §2 | `KEEP` | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-03, Wave 3): News (`BlogPost`) already had full admin CRUD (`GET/POST /cms/posts`, `GET /cms/manage/posts`, `PATCH /cms/posts/{id}`) and a working public `/news` page with an honest empty state -- no gap there, confirmed directly. The real, confirmed gap: `GalleryItem` had zero write/manage endpoints anywhere in the codebase -- only the public read (`GET /public/gallery`) and seed-time direct DB inserts existed, so an Admin could not publish a gallery item at all (`PUB-005-AC01`'s "Admin publishes via CMS" half). Added `GET /cms/manage/gallery`, `POST /cms/gallery`, `PATCH /cms/gallery/{id}`, mirroring the exact `posts` pattern (same `require_editor` division scoping, same audit-log shape) -- no schema change needed, every field already existed on the model. Added the missing "Gallery Management" Admin console section (nav entry, table, create/update action forms) -- didn't exist in any form before. Ran the full backend regression given the shared `cms.py` change -- 381/381 passed clean. Backend: 9 new pytest cases -- admin creates in own division, cross-division admin rejected, Super Admin creates in any division, update toggles `published`, unknown-item 404, non-admin rejected, auth required, the manage list shows unpublished items the public endpoint correctly hides, manage-list role gate (`test_pub_005_gallery_management.py`). E2E: 3 new Playwright cases -- Super Admin publishes a gallery item through the real UI action, a non-admin is 403'd at the API layer, unauthenticated access redirects to login (public-page-reflects-the-new-item assertion deliberately omitted -- `publicApi()`'s 60s ISR cache makes that non-deterministic within one run, a known pre-existing pattern, not this feature's own gap) |

Added `Submission.is_late` (migration `0007`, backfilled from existing rows), computed server-side on submit/resubmit, surfaced in the API response, the trainer-facing portal grid ("(late)" suffix), and the student's own confirmation message. Added `Agreement`/`ConsentRecord` models (migration `0008`), a new enrolment status `pending_consent` (new bookings start here, not `active`), and `GET/POST /workflows/it/student/agreements/current`/`{id}/accept`. Capacity accounting (`DEC-WF-002`) was carefully preserved — a `pending_consent` seat still counts against batch capacity at all 5 call sites that gate on it, so this closes the consent gap without reopening an overbooking race. The real gap was PRD-STU-007 -- folded into this same feature -- "module/course completion percentage and outstanding blockers (e.g. unfinished assignments)": the student-facing `GET /portal/it/student/attendance` section showed neither. Added `Course progress` and `Attendance` % metrics and an `Outstanding blockers` panel (unsubmitted published assignments in the student's batch) to that section, alongside the existing session table and correction-request panel. STU-006-AC02 (no session marked yet must never read as "absent") holds by construction -- `Attendance` rows only ever exist for dates a trainer actually marked, so an unmarked session is simply absent from the list, never synthesized with a status. E2E: 1 new Playwright case (76 total, 75 passing + 1 pre-existing, documented `RAID.md` I-04 contention flake unrelated to this feature) |
| `STU-007` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §3–4, `API_CONTRACT.md` §3 | `KEEP` (certificate-specific split per `TEST_REWRITE_PLAN.md` §1, `API-CERT-VER-01`–`03`) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-02, Wave 2): issuance (`ADM-006`) and the read-only `GET /portal/it/student/certificates` listing already existed. The real gap: that listing's "file" column exposed the raw, permanently-public `Certificate.file_url` through the generic table -- which only ever renders plain text, never a working link -- not the "signed, short-lived URL... never a public object link" `API_CONTRACT.md` calls for this feature specifically. Added `GET /workflows/it/certificates/{id}/download` (self-scoped, admin-accessible, derives the storage key from `certificate_no` rather than trusting stored `file_url`) and a real `CertificateDownloadPanel.tsx` action (replacing the inert table cell, same pattern as `STU-001`/`STU-003`/`STU-004`'s dedicated panels). Also found and fixed a real, adjacent bug while building this: local-storage mode's presigned URL (`/local-files/...`) is relative to the **API** container, not the web app, and nothing proxied that path -- a real browser click would have 404'd. Added `apps/web/app/local-files/[...path]/route.ts`, mirroring the existing `/api/*` proxy route, so local-mode downloads actually resolve (S3 mode, already an absolute URL, is unaffected). STU-007-AC02 (certificate unavailable before eligibility) holds by construction -- no `Certificate` row exists until issued. Backend: 6 new pytest cases -- list+download, cross-student 403, admin override-access, 404 on unknown id, empty-list-not-error, auth required (162/162 suite). E2E: 2 new Playwright cases (78 total; `adm-005`'s pre-existing `RAID.md` I-04 contention flake is the only non-pass, confirmed unrelated) |
| `STU-008` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §4.6, `API_CONTRACT.md` §3 | `KEEP` (retraces) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-02, Wave 2): `DATA_MODEL.md` §4.6 described `CourseFeedback` as "carries over" from the reference implementation, but no such table -- or any other feedback model -- existed anywhere in the codebase, same "doc says X carries over but never existed" pattern already found once for `STU-005`'s `assigned_to_user_id`. Net-new: `CourseFeedback` model (alembic `0011`), `POST /workflows/it/student/feedback` (422, not a silent drop, when no matching `Enrollment` exists for the target batch -- STU-008-AC03), a `feedback` portal section listing the student's own enrolled batches with per-batch submission status, and `FeedbackSubmissionPanel.tsx`. The PRD's own anonymous-submission option (`STU-008-AC02`) is an explicitly unconfirmed, PROPOSED-only open item (`PRD-STU-009`) -- deliberately not built; every feedback row is identified. Backend: 6 new pytest cases -- submit, reject-if-not-enrolled, rating out of 1–5 range rejected, non-student role rejected, listing reflects submission status, auth required (168/168 suite). E2E: 3 new Playwright cases (81 total; `adm-005`'s pre-existing, already-tracked `RAID.md` I-04 contention flake is the only non-pass) |
| `STU-010` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §7.2, `API_CONTRACT.md` §3 | `KEEP` (66 old `STU-*`-coded cases retrace) | COMPLETE 2026-09-03 — 17 new pytest cases in `test_pay_001_stu_010_payment_gateway.py` (unblocked: real Razorpay TEST credentials landed in `.env`) |
| `STU-011` | `API_CONTRACT.md` §3 (`DATA_MODEL.md` names no table for this feature) | `KEEP` (retraces) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-02, Wave 2): profile field editing already existed and worked (`PATCH /auth/me`, inherently self-scoped -- no id parameter exists to target another user) but had zero test evidence. Document management genuinely did not exist: `StudentDocument` is Overseas-division-specific (FK to `overseas_applications`, gated to overseas roles), not reusable for an IT student's own CV/portfolio uploads without conflating two domains. Net-new: `ProfileDocument` model (alembic `0012`), `GET/POST /workflows/it/student/profile/documents` (415/413, not a silent drop, on disallowed type/oversized file -- STU-011-AC02, reusing the exact same `_allowed()`/`max_upload_bytes` check as `/files/presign`), and `ProfileDocumentUpload.tsx` (same presign-then-upload flow as the Overseas `DocumentUpload` panel). Backend: 7 new pytest cases -- profile edit, upload+list, disallowed type rejected, oversized file rejected, self-scope isolation, non-student role rejected, auth required (175/175 suite). E2E: 3 new Playwright cases (84 total, all green) |
| `TRN-001` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §4, `API_CONTRACT.md` §4, `RBAC_MATRIX.md` | `KEEP` (old cases retrace) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-01): unlike most features so far, this one needed no code change at all -- `GET /portal/it/trainer/dashboard` already correctly listed the authenticated trainer's own batches (`Batch.trainer_id == user.id`) via the same `_payload()`/`DataTable` pattern used elsewhere, with route-level RBAC (`portal.py`'s division/role match, 403 otherwise) already deny-by-default. What was actually missing was test evidence tying that to this Feature ID. Backend: 4 new pytest cases -- cross-trainer isolation, empty state, non-trainer role rejected, auth required (79/79 suite). E2E: 2 new Playwright cases (47/47 suite). **Follow-up fix (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-05, tester feedback via WhatsApp 2026-09-04, `RAID.md` I-15):** "on DASHBOARD one table was coming outside from alignment". Reproduced directly at a 390px viewport before writing code: not the `DataTable` itself (its `.table-wrap{overflow:auto}` correctly contains its own horizontal scroll -- confirmed `document.body.scrollWidth === document.body.clientWidth` at every tested width, no page-level overflow) but the shared `PortalShell.tsx` sidebar nav, which becomes a fixed bottom bar below 640px with `overflow:auto` and zero visible affordance -- the Trainer's own 9-item nav truncated mid-word at "Mate[rials]" with a hard clipped edge, exactly matching a "misaligned" table to a non-technical reporter. `RESPONSIVE_RULES.md`'s baseline rule (`NFR-RESP-001`, `CONFIRMED_CURRENT`) requires "Primary action always reachable without horizontal scroll... collapses behind a toggle" -- this violated it directly. Fixed by reusing the exact toggle+dropdown pattern already proven for the public site's own mobile nav (`MobileNavToggle.tsx`, generalized with optional `buttonClassName`/`panelClassName`/`panelId` props, defaults unchanged so the public site's own usage is unaffected -- confirmed live) rather than inventing a new interaction. This is a shared layout component used by every role's portal, so the fix applies everywhere, not just `TRN-001`. Separately found, not fixed (out of this fix's narrow scope, flagged instead): the 640–980px tablet range renders every nav item as an unlabeled bullet ("•"), indistinguishable from each other -- a related but distinct gap, logged as `RAID.md` I-17. Tests: 3 new Playwright E2E cases (`trn-001-mobile-nav.spec.ts`) -- full nav reachable via toggle at 390px with zero body-level overflow, panel closes after navigating, desktop sidebar unaffected above the mobile breakpoint; `stu-002-dashboard.spec.ts`'s own 375px-viewport responsive case re-run clean (its unrelated "shows upcoming assignments" case failed on rerun too, in isolation -- confirmed pre-existing `RAID.md` I-03 accumulated-junk symptom, not caused by this change); `trn-001-my-batches.spec.ts` re-run clean (2/2). No backend change -- pure frontend/CSS |
| `TRN-002` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §4, `API_CONTRACT.md` §4, `DEC-WF-002` | `KEEP` (old cases retrace) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-01): `GET /workflows/it/trainer/context` already returned the trainer's own batches and active enrolments correctly scoped, but was missing schedule/status detail (start/end date, status, mode, timezone -- only id/name/schedule/capacity existed, sized for dropdown pickers elsewhere) and had zero dedicated test coverage. Extended the response, then built `BatchRosterPanel.tsx` -- each assigned batch renders as its own card with its own roster, never mixed across batches. Backend: 5 new pytest cases -- roster scoped strictly per batch, schedule/status fields present, cross-trainer isolation, non-trainer rejected, auth required (84/84 suite). E2E: 2 new Playwright cases (49/49 suite) |
| `TRN-003` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §4, `API_CONTRACT.md` §4, `DEC-LIVE-001` | `KEEP` (old cases retrace) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-01): `GET /communications/it/live-sessions` already scoped a trainer to their own batches and returned `host_url` only to trainer/admin roles (correctly hidden from students, per `STU-003`) -- but the frontend `LiveClassesPanel` always rendered `meeting_url`, ignoring `host_url` entirely, and nothing rendered it for the trainer at all. Generalized the component (`joinUrl = host_url \|\| meeting_url`, transparently correct for both roles since a student's `host_url` is always null) and added an `upcomingOnly` mode for a dashboard widget scoped to sessions not yet concluded, soonest-first, per this feature's own "Dashboard lists upcoming sessions" description. Backend: 5 new pytest cases -- host link visible to the owning trainer, cross-trainer isolation, no-link-yet represented cleanly (`TRN-003-AC02`), empty state, non-Self role rejected (89/89 suite). E2E: 2 new Playwright cases (51/51 suite) |
| `TRN-005` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §4, `API_CONTRACT.md` §4 | `KEEP` (old cases retrace) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-01): `POST /workflows/it/trainer/assignments` (create) already existed and was already used by `TeacherWorkspaceActions.tsx`'s "Create assignment" form; the entire edit half was missing -- no `PATCH` endpoint, no edit UI. Added `PATCH /workflows/it/trainer/assignments/{id}` (partial update, own-batch RBAC) and `GET /workflows/it/trainer/assignments` (own-batch list, feeds the edit picker), plus an "Edit assignment" form. `TRN-005-AC02` ("editing does not retroactively invalidate submitted work") holds by construction -- `Submission.is_late` (`STU-004`) is computed once at submission time and never recomputed from the assignment's current `due_date`; verified directly with a test that edits `due_date` after a late submission exists and confirms the stored flag is unchanged. The catalogue's one-line description also says "with... rubric," but no rubric field/entity is specified anywhere in `DATA_MODEL.md`/`API_CONTRACT.md` -- treated as descriptive language covered by the existing free-text `description` field, not a separate structured requirement to invent without evidence. Backend: 6 new pytest cases (95/95 suite). E2E: 2 new Playwright cases (53/53 suite) |
| `TRN-007` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §4, `API_CONTRACT.md` §4 | `KEEP` (old cases retrace) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-02): `PATCH /workflows/it/trainer/submissions/{id}` (grading) and the "Grade submission" UI already existed and already worked correctly -- own-batch RBAC, an `AuditLog` entry on every call, and the student-facing portal payload already surfaced `score`/`feedback` from the same row -- but had zero test coverage. `TRN-007-AC02` ("re-grading overwrites... not a duplicate record") holds by construction: grading always updates the same `Submission` row in place. Verified directly: re-graded twice, confirmed one row + two distinct audit entries. Backend: 6 new pytest cases (101/101 suite). | `ADM-006` | `API_CONTRACT.md` §5 | `KEEP` (retraces) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-02, Wave 2): issuance (`POST /workflows/it/certificates/{enrollment_id}/issue`) already existed and already worked -- eligibility check, criteria snapshot, own-batch RBAC for a Trainer -- but `ADM-006-AC02`'s own contract ("Requires override_reason... 422 without it") was not enforced: an override only ever needed a bare boolean, no written justification captured anywhere. No Admin-facing UI to issue a certificate existed at all either -- only a Trainer-side form under "student-progress". Added the `override_reason` requirement (durable on both the certificate's own `criteria_snapshot` and the `AuditLog` entry) and a new Admin-wide "certificates" portal section + `AdminCertificatePanel.tsx`. Trainer access to the same shared issuance endpoint predates this feature (no approved `TRN-*` Feature ID claims it, and removing it would break `STU-007`'s own already-shipped test coverage) -- left as-is rather than removed, an honest interpretive call on `ADM-006-AC03`'s "IT Admin" framing rather than a silent gap. Also found and fixed a real, pre-existing bug while testing the plain-409 (no-override) path directly for the first time ever: `HTTPException(409, {"criteria": criteria})` crashed with a 500, not a 409, because `criteria["batch_id"]` is a raw `UUID` and `HTTPException.detail` is serialized by Starlette's plain `json.dumps`, not FastAPI's `jsonable_encoder` -- every prior caller always passed `override=True`, so this path had never actually been exercised. Backend: 5 new pytest cases -- override requires a reason (422 without one), override reason recorded durably (certificate + audit log), Admin-wide certificates directory lists issued certificates, non-admin/non-trainer rejected, auth required (204/204 suite). E2E: 2 new Playwright cases (96 total; contention flakes per `RAID.md` I-04/I-06 continue to appear on a *different* spec each full-suite run -- confirmed each one passes in isolation). Also found and fixed a real E2E-test bug while stabilizing `TRN-006`'s own spec (not a product defect): the generic `DataTable` paginates client-side (10 rows/page) -- once accumulated test debris pushed a batch's assessment count past 10, a bare `page.locator("table")).toContainText(...)` assertion could legitimately miss a real, correctly-scheduled row sitting on page 2, and no amount of retry/polling fixes that (it's not a timing issue). Fixed by searching the table down to the run's own unique title first, same "narrow to what this run actually created" principle as every other throwaway-record spec this session -- documented as a new, generalizable lesson since it can silently affect any future spec that asserts against a `DataTable`-rendered list with a bare `toContainText` |

Added a `university_rep`-specific branch mirroring that exact precedent, checked ahead of the generic fallback. "Admission Updates" mislabeling is a real, confirmed, but *not* fixed gap -- no data model exists anywhere for a queryable per-application update feed (updates are fire-and-forget `_notify_user` notifications, not a persisted, listable record), so building a distinct view there would mean inventing an unconfirmed concept; left open. "Personal details" (also named in the same tester report) is confirmed absent -- no `profile`/`personal-details`/`settings` route exists for this role (404, not in `PORTAL_NAV`), and no requirement anywhere in `docs/` calls for staff-role self-service profile editing (only `STU-011` covers this, Student-only) -- correctly left unbuilt as new, unconfirmed scope (`PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 60), not silently added. Backend: 2 new pytest cases -- Offer Letters shows only offer-stage/offer-lettered applications with the correct title, scoped to the Rep's own institution (9/9 `test_uni_001_university_rep_portal.py`; 26/26 across the other files sharing this code path -- `test_cns_001_counselor_workspace.py`, `test_rpt_001_reporting.py`, `test_adm_009_resources_oversight.py`, `test_adm_012_roles_permissions.py`). E2E: 1 new Playwright case -- Offer Letters renders its own heading/description in the real browser (5/5 `uni-001-university-rep-portal.spec.ts`). Full regression not re-run (narrow, single-branch change; per-file targeted runs per the batching cadence). |

RBAC scoped strictly to `overseas_admin` per `RPT-002-AC03`, not extended to Counselor despite being named a secondary actor in the feature's own description -- matching this codebase's established "narrow to only what AC/RBAC confirms" convention. | `SEC-001` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §7.3, `SECURITY_CONTROLS.md` §10, `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §3 | Partial overlap (old `SEC-033`/`034` audit cases, different ID space — see `TEST_STRATEGY.md` §5 naming note) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-03, Wave 2 — unblocked by `AGT-001`+`AGT-004`, both done): `AGT-001`'s approve/reject and `AGT-004`'s payout-approval already wrote `AuditLog` rows sharing one `db.commit()` with the privileged write — fail-closed by construction, confirmed directly by code inspection *and* by a forced-failure test (monkeypatched `AuditLog` constructor) rather than assumed. The real, confirmed gap: `DATA_MODEL.md` §7.3 names `outcome` as a required `AuditLog` field ("actor_user_id, action, resource_type, resource_id, outcome, metadata, created_at") and the catalog's own SEC-001 description says the same ("...logged with actor, timestamp, outcome") — the model never had one. New migration `0020` adds `AuditLog.outcome`, set to a real business-outcome value ("approved"/"rejected"/"pending") at this feature's own named call sites (`AGT-001`'s `approve_agent`/`reject_agent`, `AGT-004`'s `approve_commission_payout`); untouched call sites elsewhere keep a neutral "recorded" default — a deliberate scope boundary matching the feature's own catalog description, not an oversight, directly tested. Also verified (not modified) that `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §3's other named privileged writes (GDPR fulfil/reject in `admin.py`'s `fulfil_data_request`, certificate override in `workflows.py`) already share one transaction with their own audit write. "Super Admin security-log export" (also named in that control row) has no distinct write action anywhere in the codebase to verify — only the existing read-only `GET /admin/audit` view — so nothing there needed auditing; not invented as new scope. Surfaced the new field in that same endpoint's response and the Super Admin "Security & Audit Logs" page's column list (verified rendering live, `curl` against the running stack). Ran the full backend regression given the shared `AuditLog` model/migration change — 365/365 passed clean. Backend: 7 new pytest cases (`SEC-AUDIT-01`) — approval/rejection/payout-approval outcomes recorded correctly, payout's two-row audit-trail ordering, fail-closed forced-failure tests for both `AGT-001` approval and `AGT-004` payout-approval (the underlying privileged write never persists), the deliberate "recorded" default confirmed for an untouched call site, the admin audit endpoint surfaces `outcome`. No new UI screen (catalog: UX required N); no new E2E spec (catalog: Required test types Unit/API only) |
| `SEC-002` | `DATA_MODEL.md` §7.4, `API_CONTRACT.md` §11, `SECURITY_CONTROLS.md` §9 | Partial overlap (old `SEC-025`/`026`/`027` privacy cases) | **COMPLETE** (`prompts/13`, 2026-09-02, Wave 2): "consent capture at data-collection points" is already satisfied by `STU-009`'s `ConsentRecord` (the enrolment agreement) — no other data-collection point is named anywhere in evidence as needing its own consent capture, so nothing new was built for that half. The genuinely net-new half is `DataSubjectRequest` (one table with a `type` discriminator, matching `API_CONTRACT.md` §11's single `POST /account/data-requests` endpoint family exactly, rather than the two same-shaped tables `DATA_MODEL.md` §7.4 names) — export is fulfilled synchronously with a real JSON export of the requester's own data, served via a signed download URL (`GET /account/data-requests/{id}/export`); delete is idempotent on `Idempotency-Key`, self-scoped (a 404 masks another user's request), and checked against the one concrete, evidence-backed retention-hold rule (`DATA_MODEL.md` §8: never hard-delete a user with an existing `Payment` or `ConsentRecord`) — rejected with a real, visible reason when blocked, queued for `IT Admin` review (`PATCH /admin/data-requests/{id}`) otherwise. Admin fulfilment performs a conservative, bounded redaction of the `User` row's own PII fields only — no cascading deletes into `Enrollment`/`Payment`/`ConsentRecord`/`AuditLog`. Exact retention periods and legal sign-off remain OPEN (`PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 15, `DEC-PRIV-001`) and are deliberately not invented — only presence of an existing hold is checked, not a numeric period. Backend: 10 new pytest cases (231/231 suite). Frontend: net-new `/account/privacy` page (shared across all roles, linked from the signed-in header — this feature has no one role's portal nav) + 4 new Playwright cases (104/104 suite) — export request + download, a real retention-hold rejection with a visible reason (not a silent failure), unauthenticated access, and header discoverability. |

created"; several unrelated events sharing one identical timestamp) was traced to `app/seed.py`
relying on `TimestampMixin`'s insert-time default for most School-domain rows while a few fields used
independent `datetime.now(UTC)` offsets — nothing kept the demo story internally consistent. Fixed in
`app/seed.py` (one shared `journey_base`, explicit staggered offsets) and, via an exactly-scoped
Core `update()` verified against a prior read-only row count before writing, in the already-running
dev database's existing demo rows for the five named seed students. This is a demo/seed-data
correction only — the timeline's own chronological-sort logic was already correct and was not
changed.

|---|---|---|---|
| `ENH-012` | `API_CONTRACT.md` §12A (5 new endpoints under `/school/students/{id}/portfolio*`), `DATA_MODEL.md`/migration `0035` (`portfolio_entries`, `portfolio_profiles`), `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.12 addendum (portfolio write/read scoping), `SECURITY_CONTROLS.md` §6A/§10 addenda, `PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 77 (Digital Portfolio half resolved) | **Not audited** (an enhancement; the old workbook was not consulted for it) | **COMPLETE for ENH-012's scope, 2026-09-23**, 11 implementation tasks merged and individually reviewed, plus a final whole-branch-review fix wave closing 6 findings (4 Important, 1 documentation, 1 bundle of 8 cheap fixes — see `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-22-enh-012-digital-portfolio-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-22-enh-012-digital-portfolio.md`, and `.superpowers/sdd/2026-09-22-enh-012-digital-portfolio/final-fix-report.md`). Fix wave: (1) built the previously-missing personal-statement inline editor in `PortfolioPanel.tsx` (writer-only, textarea + PATCH, `router.refresh()` on success); (2) fixed `description`/`personal_statement` validation to use the `clean_free_text` house precedent (permits `\n`/`\t`, blocks bidi-override characters) instead of the single-line `_no_control_characters` rule that made storing a line break in either field impossible — `title`/`organization` correctly kept the strict single-line rule; (3) fixed `PATCH .../portfolio/entries/{id}` silently discarding "clear this field" intent — now uses `payload.model_fields_set` to distinguish an explicit `null` (clears the field) from an omitted field (leaves it untouched), where the previous `if value is not None` check could never tell the two apart; (4) closed RBAC test-coverage gaps: `career_counselor`/`psychometric_team` read, `school_parent` read-200, positive writes for an assigned `school_teacher` and for `academic_team`, an out-of-portfolio `academic_team` 403, and DELETE ownership-mismatch/already-deleted coverage (DELETE had none despite being the one destructive endpoint); (5) documentation: this row, `ENHANCEMENT_BACKLOG.md`'s "who edits" framing corrected (staff/teacher-entered on the student's behalf, not student-edited — there is no student login), `PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 77's Digital Portfolio half marked resolved, `API_CONTRACT.md`/`RBAC_MATRIX.md`/`SECURITY_CONTROLS.md` addenda added; (6) a bundle of 8 cheap fixes — unused imports removed (`portfolio.py`'s `datetime`/`timezone`, the test file's unused top-level `mk_staff`), the `portfolio_profiles` migration index renamed from `ix_portfolio_profiles_student` to `ix_portfolio_profiles_school_student_id` to match the model's auto-generated name, an unknown-`section` `KeyError`→500 in `get_portfolio` guarded, an `IntegrityError`-branch `AttributeError`→500 in `update_personal_statement` guarded, `isPortfolioEntryBody`'s missing `!Array.isArray` guard added, a delete-flow double-click race in `PortfolioPanel.tsx` closed with a busy/inFlight guard + `refocus()`, and `profile_complete` (fetched but never rendered) now shown as a "Profile complete"/"Profile incomplete" line. Backend: `tests/test_enh_012_digital_portfolio.py` 41 passed (16 net-new this fix wave, baseline 25); targeted regression (`test_enh_005_scope.py`, `test_enh_005_approve.py`, `test_enh_005_concurrency.py`, `test_enh_005_hardening.py`, `test_enh_004_student_promotion.py`, run given the shared `schemas.py` change) 145 passed, 0 failed. Frontend: full `npx vitest run` 353 passed across 38 files (baseline 347/38; +6 net-new in `PortfolioPanel.test.tsx`, one pre-existing test updated to disambiguate the new "Add statement" button from the 10 per-section "Add" buttons it now shares an `/add/i` name-match with); `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0. **Not run this session:** the full backend/E2E regression suite and the Playwright `enh-012` spec (deliberately, per this project's every-3-4-features cadence and this fix wave's own scope — no e2e-relevant selectors were changed except additively: new ids `pf-statement-save-btn`/`pf-statement-textarea`/`pf-delete-btn-{id}`, existing selectors untouched). **ENH-012 is COMPLETE for this fix wave's scope.** |
